Dear Ms. Padilla and Members of the Evaluation Committee,
The University of Wyoming's College of Business has built something genuinely rare: an AACSB-accredited MBA portfolio at a public institution where resident and non-resident tuition are equal, where faculty access is direct and personal, and where the Wyoming lifestyle itself — open landscapes, the Cowboy Code of Ethics, zero traffic — is a differentiator no competitor in Denver, Salt Lake City, or Phoenix can replicate. Your challenge is not product quality. Your challenge is awareness and pipeline velocity. That is exactly where Propellant Media operates.
We have prepared a brief video proposal overview specifically for the University of Wyoming MBA engagement. We encourage you to watch it as a companion to this written response:
Record Video Walkthrough Specific To UWMBA
As a federally certified Minority Business Enterprise (MBE) through the NMSDC and a Google Premier Badge agency — recognized among the top 3% of agencies in the United States — Propellant Media brings both the technical firepower and the institutional values alignment this partnership deserves.
Why Propellant Media for the University of Wyoming MBA
Enrollment-Focused From Day One: Inquiry generation is our core discipline. Propellant Media is not a full-service branding agency. We are a paid digital media and enrollment marketing firm. Every campaign we run is built around one outcome: qualified inquiries that enter your Salesforce pipeline and move toward application.
Salesforce-Native Campaign Attribution: We have managed Salesforce-integrated enrollment campaigns for universities including Utah State University Online and Kennesaw State University. We build bi-directional attribution loops so your team can see exactly which media touches drove each applicant — not just leads.
Dual-Program Architecture Built for Your Portfolio: The UW MBA serves working professionals nationally and graduating students locally. We design campaigns that speak differently to each — separate audiences, separate creative, separate nurture cadences — within a unified budget that can scale as enrollment grows toward your 300-student online goal.
AI-Powered Targeting in a 30-Point Innovation Category: Your evaluation committee weighted strategic and innovative dimensions at 30 points — the highest category. We provide anonymous visitor identification that lets your team prioritize the prospects most likely to enroll before they ever submit an inquiry.
Graduate-Level Higher Ed Track Record: We bring three references from four-year university partnerships where we managed paid media, Salesforce attribution, and enrollment reporting. We will make those contacts available for your direct outreach.
Transparent, Flexible Pricing Built for State Budget Reality: The $50,000–$70,000 media floor is a starting point, not a ceiling. We will present a transparent, line-item fee structure with a base retainer plus a 15% media management fee — plus optional pricing for the MS Accounting and MS Finance programs — so you see exactly where every dollar goes.
We have carefully reviewed RFP # TGP-2026-07 and will provide all required submittals in accordance with the terms and conditions of this solicitation, including Exhibit A (Form of Proposal) and Exhibit B (UW Domain Name Services Amendment) where applicable.
Propellant Media shall complete all services set forth in this proposal within the proposed time limits to the satisfaction of the University of Wyoming.
Feel free to view the creative banner assets and video assets we developed below.
Thank you for your time and consideration.
Justin Croxton - CEO
Propellant Media
800 Battery Avenue SE, Suite 300
Atlanta, GA 30339
justin@propellant.media
https://propellant.media
Propellant Media
Propellant Media, LLC was founded in 2015 as a performance-driven digital marketing agency. Over the past decade, we have evolved into one of the most specialized enrollment marketing firms serving higher education institutions across the United States — managing paid media, programmatic campaigns, CRM-integrated attribution, and full-funnel analytics for universities and colleges at every tier.
We have been engaged in the business of providing the services described in this solicitation continuously since our founding. Our higher education vertical encompasses paid search, paid social, programmatic display, OTT/CTV, geofencing, Salesforce CRM integration, Looker Studio dashboards, and enrollment analytics — the exact scope UW has outlined in RFP # TGP-2026-07.
Company Overview

Ownership
Propellant Media, LLC is independently owned by Justin Croxton (CEO). There is no parent company. The firm is not publicly traded. Justin Croxton serves as the authorized signatory for all contracts and proposals.
Higher Education Experience
Propellant Media has served higher education institutions across undergraduate, graduate, and professional program enrollment since 2017. Our confirmed clients include:

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="80.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="80.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000008A"><w:pPr><w:widowControl w:val="0"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Paid media, Slate CRM integration, programmatic
For Kennesaw State University, our campaigns generated over 44,000 qualified leads annually and contributed to record-setting enrollment growth — independently verified by East Cobb News. For Utah State University Online, we delivered a blended average CPL of $79 across all channels with full Salesforce CRM attribution from first ad impression through application start.
The University of Wyoming MBA portfolio presents a clearly defined strategic challenge: two programs serving distinct audiences — one a single-cohort, on-campus 1-Year MBA targeting graduating and recent graduates, the other a rolling-admissions online Executive MBA targeting working professionals — both in need of pipeline acceleration to reach enrollment goals of 40 on-campus students and 300 total online students.
<w:left w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:bottom w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:right w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:between w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/></w:pBdr><w:spacing w:before="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:colFirst="0" w:colLast="0" w:name="_cgwmta3yjp95" w:id="0"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="0"/><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Propellant Media's strategy for the UWMBA is not a generic digital marketing playbook. It is built from three realities UW's own Q&A process revealed: (1) the current inquiry volume of 3,000–6,000 annually is inconsistently tracked, suggesting untapped pipeline; (2) nurture communications beyond the inquiry stage are minimal and disconnected from Salesforce; and (3) the highest-converting audiences are domestic working professionals and recent graduates — not the international segment that currently generates noise without yield.

This prevents audience overlap and ensures that budget pacing is tied to each program's distinct enrollment cycle — with heavier investment in the first 8-week cycles of Fall and Spring semesters, where UW's own data confirms strongest enrollment yield.

<w:left w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:bottom w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:right w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:between w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/></w:pBdr><w:spacing w:after="0" w:afterAutospacing="0" w:before="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:colFirst="0" w:colLast="0" w:name="_4te34zvbedxj" w:id="1"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="1"/><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Demand Capture Signals: Intent signals: active search queries around MBA programs, AACSB accreditation, online MBA rankings, Wyoming MBA, affordable MBA, and flexible MBA format
<w:left w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:bottom w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:right w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:between w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/></w:pBdr><w:spacing w:after="0" w:afterAutospacing="0" w:before="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:colFirst="0" w:colLast="0" w:name="_93rcb4w79ik8" w:id="2"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="2"/><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Audience Layering: Behavioral layering: LinkedIn job title targeting (Manager, Director, Senior Analyst), industry segments (energy, finance, healthcare, government — all strong in Wyoming's economy and neighboring states), and employer size filters for the online Executive program

B.2.a — Campaign Creative Concepts
Our creative concept for the UWMBA centers on a single strategic truth UW's own Q&A revealed: the Wyoming lifestyle is a differentiator. No MBA program in Denver, Salt Lake City, or Fort Collins can offer AACSB accreditation at in-state tuition rates, direct faculty access, and a daily environment defined by mountains, open space, and the Cowboy Code of Ethics. Our creative leads with that story.
Ad creative will operate in three thematic pillars:
Pillar 1: Ambition: Career momentum messaging for working professionals: "Your career doesn't wait. Neither should your MBA." Focus on ROI, flexibility, and AACSB credibility.
Pillar 2: Access: Community and access messaging for on-campus prospects: "Small cohort. Big network. Wyoming's only AACSB MBA." Focus on the 1-Year format, peer caliber, and faculty relationships.
Pillar 3: Wyoming Advantage: Lifestyle differentiation for geographic expansion: "MBA. Mountains. No commute." Focus on quality of life for professionals considering relocation or remote study.
B.2.b — Landing Pages
We strongly prefer landing pages on the uwyo.edu domain for SEO equity and ADA Title II compliance alignment. We have read UW's confirmation that external pages are unlikely to be approved as of December 2025. Our approach:
We will collaborate directly with the UW web team to design and deploy landing pages within the uwyo.edu infrastructure, providing page wireframes, copy, and conversion optimization recommendations
All pages will meet ADA Title II accessibility standards — this is a non-negotiable in our build process
Tracking pixels and UTM parameters will be coordinated with UW IT for Salesforce attribution compatibility
We do not require external subdomains to run effective campaigns — our attribution framework works within UW's domain environment
B.2.c — Ad Testing Approach
Every campaign launches with a structured A/B testing framework across headline, image/video, and CTA variants. We typically run 3–5 creative variants per ad set in the first 30 days, then eliminate underperformers and scale winners by Day 45. Creative rotation occurs every 6–8 weeks or when click-through rates decline by more than 15% from baseline.
B.2.d — Print Materials
We do not include print as a primary channel recommendation given the digital focus of this RFP and the national reach required for the Online MBA. If UW identifies specific recruiting events (graduate fairs, career fairs) where print collateral is needed, we can coordinate production through our creative team in collaboration with UW's internal design resources.
B.2.e — Creative Refresh Frequency
Standard refresh cycle: full creative refresh every 60–90 days per program. We flag creative fatigue proactively in our monthly reporting, with a recommendation memo to UWMBA staff when performance indicators signal the need for new assets ahead of schedule.
B.2.f — Resources Needed from UWMBA
Access to Canto photography library (institutional and College of Business databases)
Available B-roll video footage for YouTube and OTT placements
Written brand guidelines and College of Business design system
Program-specific value proposition priorities from Quique Girones and Ronn Smith
30-minute creative brief kickoff call within first two weeks of engagement
Sample creative banner assets
Sample video assets
B.3.a — Channel Mix Strategy
The UWMBA portfolio requires a differentiated channel approach by program. With a $50,000–$70,000 annual media budget, every dollar must earn its place. Our recommended channel mix for Year 1:

Wyoming lifestyle brand storytelling.
<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="80.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="80.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000118"><w:pPr><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">10–15%
We do not recommend OTT/CTV or audio for Year 1 at this budget level — those channels perform best at $100K+ media spend where frequency targets can be met. We will revisit if the budget scales.
B.3.a.i — UW Website & Social Media Expectations
We are not requesting website management or organic social media management — that scope is handled internally by UW. However, to maximize paid media performance, we will need:
Coordination with UW web team for landing page deployment and pixel installation on program pages
Access to review and provide conversion rate optimization (CRO) recommendations on existing MBA program pages — we will not implement changes without UW web team approval
Awareness of UW's social media posting calendar for the MBA programs so paid campaigns can amplify organic content during peak enrollment periods
B.3.b — Channel Buys, Spending Schedules & Optimization
Media is purchased through managed platform accounts (Google Ads, Meta Business Manager, LinkedIn Campaign Manager, programmatic DSP). All accounts are owned by Propellant Media with UW granted view access at any time.
Spending schedules are built around UWMBA's enrollment calendar — five annual start dates, with heaviest pacing in the 8 weeks prior to the first Fall and first Spring semester starts. Budget is front-weighted toward the strongest conversion windows and pulled back during low-yield periods (summer, mid-semester).
Optimization is continuous: we review performance data weekly, adjust bids daily through automated rules and manual intervention, and make creative recommendations monthly. No budget reallocation exceeding 20% of a channel's monthly allocation will be made without UWMBA approval.

<w:left w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:bottom w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:right w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:between w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/></w:pBdr><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:colFirst="0" w:colLast="0" w:name="_xx3w5ubfrlgb" w:id="16"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="16"/><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">UW's own Q&A identified nurture as the most significant gap in its current enrollment marketing operation: "The current nurture process is limited and minimally integrated with Salesforce." A program coordinator manages applicant inquiries manually. There is no structured pre-application nurture sequence connected to media campaign activity.
<w:left w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:bottom w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:right w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/><w:between w:color="auto" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="none"/></w:pBdr><w:spacing w:before="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:colFirst="0" w:colLast="0" w:name="_xx3w5ubfrlgb" w:id="16"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="16"/><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Propellant Media will not replicate that manual process. This eliminates the manual data entry currently required by UW's program coordinator and closes the attribution gap between media activity and Salesforce records.
B.6.a — Real-Time Performance Dashboard
Yes — Propellant Media will provide a vendor-hosted, real-time performance dashboard accessible 24/7 at login.propellant.media. This directly addresses the reporting misalignment UW identified between the College of Business's weekly Google Sheet and UW Admissions' Power BI dashboards.
Our Looker Studio-powered dashboard will serve as the single source of truth for UWMBA marketing performance. Multiple UWMBA team members can access it simultaneously without logging into individual ad platforms. The dashboard will be configured to align with the College of Business's own data definitions — not generic platform metrics.

No account manager rotation without 30 days written notice to UWMBA and client approval. In the event of unavoidable personnel transitions, we maintain a documented account playbook (channel strategy, audience configurations, creative log, Salesforce integration documentation) that ensures zero institutional knowledge loss during any transition.

It begins immediately upon contract execution. Campaigns are not delayed pending a 90-day data-sharing ramp. We build strategy from available data on Day 1 and optimize based on live performance signals.

Propellant Media's fee structure is built on the principle UW stated most consistently across its Q&A process: complete transparency and granularity. Media and agency services are priced as separate, clearly labeled line items. Nothing is bundled, and no cost appears at invoicing that is not represented in the structure below.
Annual Contract Structure — $50,000 Minimum Media Spend
The base engagement covers the two MBA programs. All line items are invoiced separately on a monthly basis.

Pre-Payment Discounts & Credits
3% discount on the annual retainer if pre-paid at the start of each contract year.
Rate lock: 15% management fee and retainer rate held constant for all 3 years — no annual escalation.
Onboarding fee waived if contract is executed by May 15, 2026.
MS program creative setup fee waived if both MS Accounting and MS Finance programs are added at contract execution.
3% reduction on incremental account management fee if both MS programs are added at contract execution.
Flexible Fee Structure
Propellant Media understands that Wyoming legislative actions and state budget cycles can create uncertainty in institutional spending. Our fee structure adapts in both directions without contract renegotiation or penalty.

No contract amendment needed.
Propellant Media's value extends beyond the eight service categories requested. The following capabilities are included at no additional cost unless otherwise noted, and each addresses a specific challenge or opportunity UW identified through its own Q&A process.
AI-Powered Prospect Engagement Scoring
Beyond standard lead generation, Propellant Media will implement AI-driven engagement scoring across the UWMBA prospect database. Every prospect who interacts with a paid ad, visits a program page, or submits an RFI receives a behavioral score updated in real time based on:
Ad click frequency and recency
Program page visit depth and return visit patterns
Video view completion rate (YouTube, OTT)
Email open and click behavior within nurture sequences
Application stage progression signals from Salesforce
Scores are surfaced directly to UWMBA's program coordinator through a simple, readable priority list — not buried in a platform dashboard. The output is actionable: a ranked list of prospects, refreshed weekly, showing who has the highest likelihood of converting if contacted today. This directly addresses UW's stated challenge of excessive international applications consuming staff time without yielding enrollment. The scoring model deprioritizes low-converting segments automatically, so staff energy concentrates on domestic prospects with genuine enrollment intent.
How it connects to Salesforce: Engagement scores are passed back into Salesforce contact records via our BigQuery + Zapier integration, enabling the program coordinator to trigger outreach directly from within the CRM workflow they already use. No new platform to log into.
Dynamic Creative Optimization (DCO)
Rather than running static ad sets, we deploy dynamic creative frameworks that automatically test combinations of headlines, images, and calls-to-action — surfacing the winning combinations based on live performance data. For the UWMBA's two distinct audiences (on-campus 1-Year MBA prospects and online Executive MBA working professionals), this means:
Different creative messages reaching different demographics automatically, without requiring UWMBA staff to manually produce or approve dozens of individual ad variants
Wyoming lifestyle messaging tested against career advancement messaging in real time — letting performance data, not assumptions, determine which angle resonates with which segment
Creative refresh cycles driven by performance signals rather than arbitrary timelines — we rotate creative when click-through rates drop, not on a fixed calendar
This is particularly valuable for the Online Executive MBA, where UW confirmed five distinct annual start dates. DCO allows us to adjust messaging emphasis by enrollment cycle without a full creative rebuild each time.
Performance Max — AI-Driven Cross-Channel Delivery
Google's Performance Max format uses AI to serve ads across Search, Display, YouTube, Gmail, and Maps from a single campaign — optimizing delivery in real time based on conversion signals from your Salesforce pipeline. For UWMBA's national reach goals (specifically the 50% non-resident online enrollment target), Performance Max expands geographic reach with automated channel prioritization.
We deploy Performance Max strategically alongside — not instead of — manually managed campaigns. This is an important distinction: pure Performance Max campaigns surrender visibility and control. Our approach preserves manual control over budget allocation and creative messaging while using Performance Max to capture incremental reach in geographies and audiences that manual campaigns miss.
Anonymous Website Visitor Identification
UW confirmed in Q31 that no mechanism currently exists to identify anonymous website visitors before they self-identify. This is a significant untapped opportunity for the Online Executive MBA specifically: working professionals researching graduate programs do extensive anonymous browsing before they ever fill out an RFI. The average B2B buyer conducts 12+ digital research touchpoints before raising their hand — MBA prospects behave similarly.
Propellant Media can implement an anonymous visitor identification layer on MBA program pages (coordinated with UW IT and UW web team for ADA Title II compliance and data governance). This reveals:
Company name and industry of visiting organizations — directly applicable to Executive MBA audience targeting of working professionals at regional employers in Wyoming, Colorado, Utah, and Montana
Geographic location of anonymous visitors — informing which states are generating organic interest that paid campaigns can amplify
Pages visited and session duration — signaling program-specific interest before an RFI is submitted
All data is stored within the United States, consistent with UW's Q68 requirement. This feeds directly into our programmatic retargeting campaigns, turning anonymous interest into addressable audiences before a prospect ever identifies themselves.
HALDA AI Chatbot Optimization for College of Business Programs
UW confirmed that HALDA AI is currently deployed on admissions pages but is not optimized for College of Business graduate program recruiting. The current chatbot cannot accurately answer common MBA questions, does not capture RFI information, and does not route qualified leads to the Graduate Programs coordinator — meaning prospects who engage with it either leave without converting or create manual work for staff.
Propellant Media will provide HALDA configuration recommendations as part of the base engagement at no additional cost, including:
Program-specific Q&A scripting covering the most frequently asked questions for the 1-Year MBA and Online Executive MBA (admissions requirements, AACSB accreditation, tuition structure, start dates, work experience expectations)
RFI capture integration — triggering an RFI form within the HALDA conversation when a prospect indicates enrollment intent, rather than directing them to a separate page where drop-off is higher
Lead routing setup — ensuring chatbot-generated inquiries flow into Salesforce with proper source attribution, so they appear in the program coordinator's pipeline alongside paid media leads
International prospect filtering — chatbot-level language that surfaces relevant information about domestic enrollment requirements, reducing the volume of international inquiries that reach the program coordinator without enrollment intent
Based on chatbot implementation data from comparable graduate programs, a properly configured enrollment chatbot typically deflects 30–40% of common inquiry questions from staff while increasing RFI completion rates for engaged visitors.
AI Governance & Disclosure Policy
UW asked about AI tools in Q102 and Q103. Our answer is straightforward.
Propellant Media uses AI tools to enhance campaign performance — audience modeling, predictive bidding, dynamic creative testing, engagement scoring, and reporting automation. We do not use AI to replace human judgment on strategy, creative direction, client communication, or budget decisions.
Every media plan is reviewed and approved by a human account lead (Alyssa Dixon) before any spend is committed. Every AI-generated audience recommendation is reviewed against enrollment data by Aaron King before activation. AI surfaces options — humans make the call.
We are happy to provide UW with a written AI usage disclosure as part of the final contract if requested.
Certifications & Designations
NMSDC Federally Certified Minority Business Enterprise (MBE) — aligned with UW's Equal Employment Opportunity commitments and affirmative action obligations
Google Premier Badge — Top 3% of U.S. Google Agencies (2024–2025)
Meta Business Partner
LinkedIn Marketing Solutions Partner

The Challenge
Propellant Media was selected through a competitive RFP process to support the launch of the Behavioral Health Workforce Collaborative — a statewide initiative uniting A-B Tech, Forsyth Tech, and Stanly Community College. The campaign needed to generate qualified leads quickly for newly launched programs across five NC counties, reaching a non-traditional adult learner audience.
Strategy & Tactics
Google Search: Campaigns targeting career-specific queries — 'behavioral health training NC,' 'substance abuse counselor certification,' 'mental health technician program near me' — with exact and phrase match segmentation
Performance Max: Structured asset groups by career pathway to expand reach beyond keyword-only targeting while controlling CPL
Meta + Site Retargeting: Adults interested in healthcare careers targeted via Meta prospecting; dynamic retargeting re-engaged program page visitors who hadn't yet converted
Geo-Targeted Delivery: Fenced to Buncombe, Madison, Forsyth, Surry, and Stanly counties with radius expansion into adjacent DMA zones

The Challenge
KSU's Fintech graduate program needed to reach finance and tech professionals considering a master's degree while maintaining lead quality and cost efficiency against competing national and online-first programs.
Strategy & Tactics
Google Ads & Performance Max: Bid strategies progressed from Max CPC to Target CPA as conversion data matured; campaigns organized by program audience cluster
Meta with Enhanced Instant Forms: Custom slider confirmation and intent-screening questions improved lead quality before prospects reached the admissions team
LinkedIn: Targeted working professionals by job function, seniority, and industry with separate creative per program audience
CRM Integration: All platforms integrated with KSU's CRM for real-time lead delivery and unified attribution dashboards from first click through application start
OTT/CTV: Top-of-funnel video reinforced program awareness among finance professionals on streaming platforms

In 2025, Propellant Media's campaign accounted for 75% of all applications generated for the Masters in Fintech Program — direct proof of enrollment influence, and a benchmark for University of Wyoming's stated priority of qualified leads that actually matriculate.
Case Study 3: Utah State University — Online Program Enrollment (2025 - Current)
This case study demonstrates Propellant Media's ability to build a full-funnel, multi-platform digital strategy for a large public university targeting adult learners across a broad, dispersed geography — directly relevant to University of Wyoming's need for an experienced media partner who can execute precisely within a defined service area while reaching multiple audience segments simultaneously.

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="cccccc" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="f5f5f5" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="160.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="160.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000316"><w:pPr><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:color w:val="1a1a1a"/><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:color w:val="1a1a1a"/><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Regionalized targeting — Utah, Colorado, Idaho, and California — with program-level geo-segmentation across each market
Utah State University's online division needed to grow enrollment among adult learners dispersed across multiple states — a geographically distributed audience with highly specific motivations: affordability, scheduling flexibility, and direct career relevance. The primary target was adults aged 25–54 with some college but no degree, a segment that is notoriously difficult to convert through broad digital advertising because their decision timeline is long and their trust threshold is high.
USU's prior outreach lacked the audience segmentation depth and cross-channel attribution infrastructure needed to connect media spend to actual enrollment outcomes. Campaign data lived in silos across platforms, making it impossible to understand which channels, creative, or messages were actually driving applications. USU needed a media partner who could build integrated full-funnel visibility from awareness through application start — and do it at scale across a four-state region.
Strategy & Tactics
Google Search & Performance Max: High-intent demand capture through program-specific keyword strategies — targeting adult learners actively searching for degree completion, online programs, and career advancement opportunities across Utah, Colorado, Idaho, and California. Performance Max asset groups structured by career pathway to expand reach beyond keyword-only targeting while maintaining CPL control. AI-powered Dynamic Search Ads layered in to capture long-tail queries Propellant's team identified through search term analysis.
Meta (Facebook & Instagram): Mid-funnel brand trust and awareness campaigns targeting adults 25–54 interested in career advancement and online education. Custom audience segments built from USU's existing inquiry data, with Lookalike Audience expansion across each state market. Creative tested across program benefit messaging, affordability positioning, and flexibility themes — optimized weekly based on lead quality signals from the CRM.
Site Retargeting: Cross-platform retargeting re-engaged program page visitors who had not yet converted. Dynamic retargeting served program-specific creative based on pages visited — ensuring that a visitor who browsed the business program saw business-specific messaging, not a generic USU ad. This drove application starts from prospects who were previously undecided.
Salesforce CRM Integration & Attribution: All campaign platforms integrated with USU's Salesforce CRM through UTM-coded asset tagging and multi-touch attribution dashboards built in Looker Studio. This gave USU's marketing team real-time visibility from first ad impression through inquiry submission and application start — enabling data-driven budget decisions and program-level CPL reporting across every channel.

<w:tblPr><w:tblStyle w:val="Table24"/><w:tblW w:w="9345.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:jc w:val="left"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:right w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:insideH w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:insideV w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/></w:tblBorders><w:tblLayout w:type="fixed"/><w:tblLook w:val="0600"/></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9345"/><w:tblGridChange w:id="0"><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9345"/></w:tblGrid></w:tblGridChange></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="0"/><w:trHeight w:val="1125" w:hRule="atLeast"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="12" w:val="single"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="dddddd" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="fdf3f1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="180.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="180.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000347"><w:pPr><w:spacing w:line="240" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Poppins" w:cs="Poppins" w:eastAsia="Poppins" w:hAnsi="Poppins"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Propellant Media is a digital marketing services agency — not a software or technology platform vendor. The services delivered under this contract (paid media management, campaign strategy, creative production, and analytics reporting) do not introduce proprietary software or technology systems to UW's infrastructure. Per the guidance in Q49 and Q70 of UW's published Q&A, a formal VPAT is not applicable to a services-led engagement of this type.
1.  Section 508 Compliance
All campaign assets produced by Propellant Media — including digital ads, landing page recommendations, and email templates — are built to WCAG 2.1 AA standards, which meets or exceeds Section 508 requirements. Specific practices include:
Color contrast ratios meeting WCAG 2.1 AA minimums on all creative assets
Alt text applied to all images in ads, email templates, and landing page wireframes
Keyboard-navigable elements on all landing page recommendations submitted to UW's web team
All assets deployed on uwyo.edu reviewed and approved by UW web team before going live
2.  Areas Not Currently Compliant
Not applicable. Propellant Media does not deploy a standalone software product. All deliverables are services-based. Any technology components coordinated with UW IT — tracking pixels, UTM parameters, dashboard access — are reviewed for ADA Title II compliance before deployment and are not implemented without written approval from UW IT and the UW web team.
3.  Accessibility Conformance Testing Process
All creative assets and landing page recommendations are reviewed against WCAG 2.1 AA standards prior to delivery. This review is conducted internally by Propellant Media's creative and account team before any asset is submitted to UWMBA for approval. Assets requiring deployment on uwyo.edu are then submitted to the UW web team for their independent compliance review before going live. No asset is deployed without both reviews completed.
4.  Upgrade Assurance
Propellant Media's primary reporting tool is Google's Looker Studio, a third-party platform maintained and updated by Google. Platform upgrades are governed by Google's own accessibility commitments. Propellant Media will:
Monitor material platform changes that could affect the accessibility of UW-facing dashboards or reporting tools
Notify UW's day-to-day contact within 5 business days of any significant platform change affecting deliverables
Conduct a full accessibility review of all UW-facing deliverables following significant upgrades before resuming normal reporting cadence
5.  Responsibility for Remediation
Propellant Media will remediate any accessibility issues in deliverables we produce at no additional cost to UW and within 10 business days of identification. For accessibility issues arising from third-party platforms (Google, Meta, LinkedIn), remediation is governed by those vendors' terms of service. Propellant Media will serve as the liaison to escalate and resolve those issues on UW's behalf, keeping UWMBA informed throughout the resolution process.
6.  VPAT / Accessibility Statement
A formal VPAT is not applicable to this engagement per Q49 and Q70 of UW's published Q&A. Propellant Media's accessibility commitment is as follows:
All deliverables produced by Propellant Media will conform to WCAG 2.1 AA standards.
All assets deployed on uwyo.edu will meet ADA Title II compliance requirements.
No tracking scripts, pixels, or tools will be implemented without prior review and written approval from UW IT and the UW web team.
For accessibility questions or remediation requests, contact:
